# Decision log

This page lists the decisions in Bombadil's design briefs, one row each, grouped into thirteen areas and numbered from D-001. Each row gives what was decided, the reason the brief gives, whether the person confirmed it, the section of the brief that holds the full text and how far it is built. A decision that a later brief replaced stays in the log, marked as superseded and pointing to the row that replaced it, so the history can be followed. The briefs in [`design/`](design/README.md) hold the reasoning in full, the [roadmap](roadmap.md) says where each piece stands today, and the [glossary](glossary.md) defines the terms used here.

## How to read it

Every table has the same seven columns.

| Column | Holds |
| --- | --- |
| ID | `D-001` and up. An ID never changes once committed, and it is never reused. |
| Date | The day the decision was made in its brief or, for a confirmed decision, the day the person confirmed it; "undated" where the brief gives no date. Rows run by date within an area, undated rows last. |
| Decision | The decision in the brief's own words. A row that replaced an earlier one starts with "Replaces D-nnn:". |
| Why | The reason the brief gives. A note that reads "Extended by", "Narrowed by" or "Changed in part by" names a later row that adds to, narrows or changes part of this one without replacing it. |
| Status | One of the five below. |
| Source | The section of the brief that holds the decision. Where several briefs state the same decision it has one row that lists every section. |
| Built | How far the decision is built, with a link to the architecture page or file that holds it. |

| Status | Means |
| --- | --- |
| Confirmed | The person answered the question and said yes. |
| Default | The brief picked it with a reason and a way out; the person has not ruled on it. |
| Open | The brief leaves the question for the person to answer. |
| Cut | Considered and left out; the Why says why. |
| Superseded by D-nnn | A later decision replaced this one. The row stays. |

The words in the Built column are the roadmap's, and each links to it.

| Built | Means |
| --- | --- |
| [Shipped](roadmap.md) | On `main`, with a test or a documented check. |
| [Partly shipped](roadmap.md) | Some of what the decision names is on `main`. |
| [In progress](roadmap.md) | On a branch or in an open pull request, not on `main`. |
| [Designed](roadmap.md) | Decided, with no code. |
| n/a | Nothing to build on its own: a cut idea, an open question or a rule that applies to everything. |

Built is the state the brief reported for that decision, read against the `main` this documentation was verified at and the unmerged branches on the remote on 2026-10-01. A brief can lag the code, so work that was on a branch when a brief was written may be on `main` now: the first two pieces of the poor man switch were merged into `main` on 2026-10-01, after that check, so their rows read In progress or Designed until the roadmap and the architecture page are verified against the merge. The [roadmap](roadmap.md) is the live record, and where the two differ it is the one to believe.

## How to add a decision

1. Write the decision in the brief that owns it, under a heading that will not change, with its reason and what was not chosen. When it changes an earlier decision, say so under "Extends or changes the earlier briefs" in the new brief.
2. Add a row at the foot of the table for its area, with the next free ID: the highest number in this file plus one.
3. Fill in the date, the decision in the brief's words, the reason, the status and a link to the brief's section.
4. If it replaces an earlier decision, change that row's Status to "Superseded by D-nnn" and begin the new Decision with "Replaces D-nnn:". If it only adds to, narrows or changes part of an earlier one, add that to the earlier row's Why.
5. Set Built from the [roadmap](roadmap.md), link the page or file that holds it, and run `python3 docs/tools/check_docs.py docs/decisions.md`.

An ID never changes once it is committed. A row is not deleted or renumbered when its decision is replaced; its Status changes and the row stays.

| ID | Date | Decision | Why | Status | Source | Built |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| <a id="d-276"></a>D-276 | 2026-09-27 | Each vendor's official agent CLI (Claude Code headless, Codex CLI) is the engine, run under Bombadil's own daemon agentd, which starts at login as the session's core. | A normal Claude or ChatGPT login works as is, and vendor improvements arrive for free. | Confirmed | [foundation-choices: 4. How the agent runs as the OS → CLIs: official agent CLIs as the engine, our own daemon around them](design/foundation-choices.md#4-how-the-agent-runs-as-the-os--clis-official-agent-clis-as-the-engine-our-own-daemon-around-them) | [Shipped](roadmap.md) · [agentd.py](../src/bombadil/agentd.py) |
| <a id="d-277"></a>D-277 | 2026-09-27 | agentd owns the conversation, the UI connection and snapshots; the vendor CLI does the thinking and tool use. | The CLIs give the best tool use each vendor offers, and Bombadil owns less code. | Confirmed | [foundation-choices: 4. How the agent runs as the OS → CLIs: official agent CLIs as the engine, our own daemon around them](design/foundation-choices.md#4-how-the-agent-runs-as-the-os--clis-official-agent-clis-as-the-engine-our-own-daemon-around-them) | [Shipped](roadmap.md) · [agentd.py](../src/bombadil/agentd.py) |
| <a id="d-278"></a>D-278 | 2026-09-27 | The agent runs as the user with passwordless sudo and the CLIs in their full-auto mode (Claude Code bypassPermissions, Codex full access), matching 'no guards'. | It matches the no-guards goal; the safety net is the btrfs snapshot per turn. | Confirmed | [foundation-choices: 4. ## Memory and the brain

| <a id="d-815"></a>D-815 | 2026-09-27 | The "Welcome back. Since last time you built Passwords and Tracker." line is built locally from turns.jsonl with no model call, and "what do you know about me?" opens a short list the person can edit. | Extended by [D-1028](#d-1028), [D-148](#d-148). | Default | [ux-brief: A week later](design/ux-brief.md#a-week-later) | [In progress](roadmap.md) |
| <a id="d-816"></a>D-816 | 2026-09-27 | One file, ~/.bombadil/memory.md, is linked as ~/.claude/CLAUDE.md and as ~/.codex/AGENTS.md; no ~/CLAUDE.md is added. | Claude runs from $HOME and would load the file twice. Extended by [D-818](#d-818). | Default | [ux-brief: A week later](design/ux-brief.md#a-week-later) | [Designed](roadmap.md) |
| <a id="d-817"></a>D-817 | 2026-09-27 | Why is this like this? (signature): "Why is my DNS set to 1.1.1.1?" gets an answer with the turn that changed it, the time and the old value, plus "Undo just this file". | It is cheap once receipts exist, and the agent can answer from turns.jsonl before any index is built. Extended by [D-181](#d-181). | Default | [ux-brief: A week later](design/ux-brief.md#a-week-later) | [Designed](roadmap.md) |
| <a id="d-818"></a>D-818 | 2026-09-27 | memory.md as ~/.claude/CLAUDE.md and ~/.codex/AGENTS.md is kept, and because every coding session now loads it, it must stay about the person; the machine's rules stay in the system prompt and the developer note reaches sessions only. |  | Confirmed | [dev-brief: Extends or changes in the first brief](design/dev-brief.md#extends-or-changes-in-the-first-brief) | [Designed](roadmap.md) |
| <a id="d-819"></a>D-819 | 2026-09-27 | Focus opens by default, the Map is the overview, and Time runs along the bottom of both, all reading one index. The free-floating force graph is not built as its own view, but the Map keeps its look. | At real size the global graph is a hairball, reshuffles every time it opens, gives lines no reason or date, and cannot be searched by looking. | Confirmed | [brain-brief: Graph, or something better](design/brain-brief.md#graph-or-something-better)<br>[brain-brief: Decisions I picked a default for](design/brain-brief.md#decisions-i-picked-a-default-for) | [Partly shipped](roadmap.md) · [brain.md](architecture/brain.md) |
| <a id="d-820"></a>D-820 | 2026-09-27 | Nobody writes the brain. The machine does, from what actually happened: every link is an event the OS witnessed, so there is nothing to tend. | Every link is an event the OS witnessed, so nothing can go stale. | Confirmed | [brain-brief: The rules](design/brain-brief.md#the-rules) | [Partly shipped](roadmap.md) · [brain.md](architecture/brain.md) |
| <a id="d-821"></a>D-821 | 2026-09-27 | Every link says why and when, for example "Downloaded from rent-portal while you read Lease renewal 2026, Tue 14:02". | A link with no reason is a guess, and guesses are what turn a graph into noise. | Confirmed | [brain-brief: The rules](design/brain-brief.md#the-rules) | [Partly shipped](roadmap.md) · [brain.md](architecture/brain.md) |
| <a id="d-822"></a>D-822 | 2026-09-27 | Plain files stay the truth: folders are still folders, every tool and both agents still read them, and the brain is an index beside them that can be rebuilt. | Deleting the brain loses the view, not the person's data or its history. | Confirmed | [brain-brief: The rules](design/brain-brief.md#the-rules)<br>[brain-brief: Decisions I picked a default for](design/brain-brief.md#decisions-i-picked-a-default-for) | [Partly shipped](roadmap.md) · [brain.md](architecture/brain.md) |
| <a id="d-823"></a>D-823 | 2026-09-27 | Things hold still: a thing keeps its place on the map from the day it appears, through renames and moves. | People learn where things are the way they learn a city. | Confirmed | [brain-brief: The rules](design/brain-brief.md#the-rules) | [Designed](roadmap.md) · [brain.md](architecture/brain.md) |
| <a id="d-824"></a>D-824 | 2026-09-27 | Bright means alive: brightness is how recently and how often something was touched, by the person or an agent. Nothing is removed for being old, it only dims, and the map at rest shows a few hundred things. |  | Confirmed | [brain-brief: The rules](design/brain-brief.md#the-rules) | [Designed](roadmap.md) · [brain.md](architecture/brain.md) |
| <a id="d-825"></a>D-825 | 2026-09-27 | What a model writes is pinned to what it read: a summary carries a fingerprint of its sources, greys out when a source changes and is rewritten the next time someone looks. | This is the fix for the folder of markdown that goes stale. | Confirmed | [brain-brief: The rules](design/brain-brief.md#the-rules) | [Partly shipped](roadmap.md) · [brain.md](architecture/brain.md) |
| <a id="d-826"></a>D-826 | 2026-09-27 | The person and the agents see the same brain: the pill, the Brain window, the machine's agent, coding sessions and apps all ask one index. | What the machine knows and what the person can see never disagree. | Confirmed | [brain-brief: The rules](design/brain-brief.md#the-rules) | [Partly shipped](roadmap.md) · [brain.md](architecture/brain.md) |
| <a id="d-827"></a>D-827 | 2026-09-27 | The brain is built as six pieces in order: the index that writes itself, Focus, find from the pill, agents on the same brain, the Map, then Time. The first starts recording history, so it starts earliest. | History that was not recorded cannot be recovered later, and a map needs a few weeks of history to be worth looking at. | Confirmed | [brain-brief: Build these first](design/brain-brief.md#build-these-first) | [Partly shipped](roadmap.md) · [brain.md](architecture/brain.md) |

| <a id="d-844"></a>D-844 | 2026-09-27 | Links are made by, changed by, came from, lives in, asked about and mentions (strong), and used with and read with (weak, shown only in Focus), each tied to the event that makes it. | Extended by [D-880](#d-880). | Confirmed | [brain-brief: What the brain is made of](design/brain-brief.md#what-the-brain-is-made-of) | [Partly shipped](roadmap.md) · [brain.md](architecture/brain.md) |
| <a id="d-845"></a>D-845 | 2026-09-27 | Every link keeps when it first and last happened, how many times, and the events behind it. | So "why" is always a click away. | Confirmed | [brain-brief: What the brain is made of](design/brain-brief.md#what-the-brain-is-made-of) | [Partly shipped](roadmap.md) · [brain.md](architecture/brain.md) |
| <a id="d-846"></a>D-846 | 2026-09-27 | Names come from folders, projects, page titles and the person's own words, with no model. |  | Confirmed | [brain-brief: What the brain is made of](design/brain-brief.md#what-the-brain-is-made-of) | [Partly shipped](roadmap.md) · [brain.md](architecture/brain.md) |
| <a id="d-847"></a>D-847 | 2026-09-27 | Only witnessed events make links. The model never invents one; the agent adds one only when asked ("link this to the lease"), recorded as the person's, and [[links]] and URLs in the person's own files count. | Links guessed from similar content are the main source of noise in a graph and are wrong often enough to make every line suspect. | Confirmed | [brain-brief: Decisions I picked a default for](design/brain-brief.md#decisions-i-picked-a-default-for) | [Partly shipped](roadmap.md) · [brain.md](architecture/brain.md) |
| <a id="d-848"></a>D-848 | 2026-09-27 | Search is a word index (FTS5 with trigrams over names, titles, extracted text and prompts), with the agent as the layer that understands meaning by rephrasing and asking the index several times. No embedding model ships for now. | A local embedding model costs disk, memory and CPU on every write for a gain that has not been shown yet. | Confirmed | [brain-brief: Decisions I picked a default for](design/brain-brief.md#decisions-i-picked-a-default-for) | [Partly shipped](roadmap.md) · [brain.md](architecture/brain.md) |
| <a id="d-849"></a>D-849 | 2026-09-27 | Bombadil builds an index of its own instead of reusing GNOME's LocalSearch or KDE's Baloo, borrowing their extractors where useful, such as pdftotext for PDFs. | Both crawl folders, and neither knows who wrote a file, which turn made it, which page it came from or what was open beside it. | Confirmed | [brain-brief: Decisions I picked a default for](design/brain-brief.md#decisions-i-picked-a-default-for) | [Partly shipped](roadmap.md) · [brain.md](architecture/brain.md) |
| <a id="d-850"></a>D-850 | 2026-09-27 | The brain reads names, metadata, the first 64 KB of text, PDF text, and EXIF date and camera but not location. Office documents come later. | Everything would mean a slow first index and secrets in the index; names only would make search miss the lease. | Confirmed | [brain-brief: Decisions I picked a default for](design/brain-brief.md#decisions-i-picked-a-default-for) | [Partly shipped](roadmap.md) · [brain.md](architecture/brain.md) |
| <a id="d-851"></a>D-851 | 2026-09-27 | The brain keeps the URL, title, time and how long the person stayed, from Chromium's History, and page text only for pages an agent read in a turn or the person asked it to remember. Narrowed by [D-890](#d-890). | Confirmed | [brain-brief: Decisions I picked a default for](design/brain-brief.md#decisions-i-picked-a-default-for) | [Partly shipped](roadmap.md) · [brain.md](architecture/brain.md) |
| <a id="d-852"></a>D-852 | 2026-09-27 | A model writes about a thing only when someone looks and there is nothing yet, using the fast model, pinned to a fingerprint and greyed when out of date; never a background crawl, never for private things. | Nightly summaries of everything would spend tokens and be stale by the morning anyway. | Confirmed | [brain-brief: Decisions I picked a default for](design/brain-brief.md#decisions-i-picked-a-default-for) | [Shipped](roadmap.md) · [brain.md](architecture/brain.md) |

| <a id="d-1007"></a>D-1007 | 2026-09-30 | The facts in the lines are the dev brief's and the first brief's own sentences, reused. |  | Default | [voice-brief: The words](design/voice-brief.md#the-words) | [In progress](roadmap.md) · [voice.md](architecture/voice.md) |
| <a id="d-1008"></a>D-1008 | 2026-09-30 | An empty place is two short sentences at most, first what is empty, naming the place, then why or what fills it with a real number or date, then exactly one doorway; no name, hello, exclamation mark or voice variants. | Empty places teach, agents and docs read them, and a copied app should not carry one person's tone. | Default | [voice-brief: Empty places](design/voice-brief.md#empty-places) | [In progress](roadmap.md) · [voice.md](architecture/voice.md) |
| <a id="d-1009"></a>D-1009 | 2026-09-30 | An empty (nothing yet) is worded, but a miss (nothing matches) and an error keep their plain wording, so The brain is not running yet stays as it is. |  | Default | [voice-brief: Empty places](design/voice-brief.md#empty-places) | n/a |
| <a id="d-1010"></a>D-1010 | 2026-09-30 | An empty place appears only where the empty thing is and leaves when it is not empty; only asked-for cards and views get one, and a widget with nothing to say is absent. |  | Default | [voice-brief: Empty places](design/voice-brief.md#empty-places) | [Designed](roadmap.md) |
| <a id="d-1011"></a>D-1011 | 2026-09-30 | An empty-place line uses a weekday (on Tuesday) for the last six days and a date after that. | Tuesday is wrong after a week. | Default | [voice-brief: Empty places](design/voice-brief.md#empty-places) | [Designed](roadmap.md) |
| <a id="d-1012"></a>D-1012 | 2026-09-30 | A chip that has nothing behind it, for example claude acme with no folder in ~/Projects, is not shown. | A suggestion the machine cannot back is filler. | Default | [voice-brief: Empty places](design/voice-brief.md#empty-places) | [Designed](roadmap.md) |
| <a id="d-1013"></a>D-1013 | 2026-09-30 | The voice reaches greetings, goodbyes and the agent's own closing sentence; the working line, receipts, errors, limits and empty places stay plain in every voice. | Greetings only would lose the character at the first ask; voicing everything would stop the working line being a plain record. | Default | [voice-brief: Decisions I picked a default for](design/voice-brief.md#decisions-i-picked-a-default-for)<br>[voice-brief: Cut, and why](design/voice-brief.md#cut-and-why) | [In progress](roadmap.md) · [voice.md](architecture/voice.md) |
| <a id="d-1014"></a>D-1014 | 2026-09-30 | Bombadil does not ask what to call itself: the OS is called Bombadil and says I or nothing, and each voice decides I or nothing. | A renamed agent would touch every error line, brief and doc for no gain. | Cut | [voice-brief: Decisions I picked a default for](design/voice-brief.md#decisions-i-picked-a-default-for) | n/a |
| <a id="d-1015"></a>D-1015 | 2026-09-30 | There are three voices, Merry, Plain and Quiet; four (with a dry deadpan one) and two (a chatty-or-quiet switch) were rejected, and a warm reassuring voice is out. | They differ on one axis each and can be judged by reading a sample. | Default | [voice-brief: Decisions I picked a default for](design/voice-brief.md#decisions-i-picked-a-default-for) | [In progress](roadmap.md) · [voice.md](architecture/voice.md) |
| <a id="d-1016"></a>D-1016 | 2026-09-30 | Name and voice live in ~/.config/bombadil/persona.toml, separate from config.toml and memory.md; what do you know about me shows the name as its first row, read from that file. | config.toml is rewritten whole by save_user, and memory.md is loaded by every coding session, where a merry voice would leak into code reviews. | Default | [voice-brief: Decisions I picked a default for](design/voice-brief.md#decisions-i-picked-a-default-for) | [In progress](roadmap.md) · [voice.md](architecture/voice.md) |
| <a id="d-1017"></a>D-1017 | 2026-09-30 | After a short break a greeting says only the facts, with no hello and no name, and only when there are facts. | Greeting someone after every coffee makes the name a tic by the fifth time. | Default | [voice-brief: Decisions I picked a default for](design/voice-brief.md#decisions-i-picked-a-default-for) | [In progress](roadmap.md) · [voice.md](architecture/voice.md) |
| <a id="d-1018"></a>D-1018 | 2026-09-30 | Speech is out of scope for the voice: it is writing only, and a greeting that speaks, or a chime, belongs with the speech service and the sound defaults of the earlier briefs. |  | Default | [voice-brief: Decisions I picked a default for](design/voice-brief.md#decisions-i-picked-a-default-for) | n/a |
| <a id="d-1019"></a>D-1019 | 2026-09-30 | No sliders and no long list of tones; named voices are chosen in one glance. | Sliders invite tuning instead of choosing, and multiply the strings to write and test. | Cut | [voice-brief: Cut, and why](design/voice-brief.md#cut-and-why) | n/a |
| <a id="d-1020"></a>D-1020 | 2026-09-30 | No Warm, Dry or Steward voice. | Warm's reassurances are claims no receipt backs, Dry's jokes are hard to keep off failures by rule, and Steward is Plain with longer words. | Cut | [voice-brief: Cut, and why](design/voice-brief.md#cut-and-why) | n/a |
| <a id="d-1021"></a>D-1021 | 2026-09-30 | No model-written greeting. | It breaks the 200 ms rule, fails offline at boot and costs tokens for eight seconds of text. | Cut | [voice-brief: Cut, and why](design/voice-brief.md#cut-and-why) | n/a |
| <a id="d-1022"></a>D-1022 | 2026-09-30 | No standing welcome, no splash, and no clock or weather on the rest screen. | The rest state is wallpaper and a pill, and the desk brief already cut clock, weather and standing cards. | Cut | [voice-brief: Cut, and why](design/voice-brief.md#cut-and-why) | n/a |
| <a id="d-1023"></a>D-1023 | 2026-09-30 | No unasked lines at other times: an evening recap, an idle nudge, an hourly check-in, tips of the day, streaks or day 30 with Bombadil. | The agent never starts work on its own, and Time is the one place for what happened. | Cut | [voice-brief: Cut, and why](design/voice-brief.md#cut-and-why) | n/a |
| <a id="d-1024"></a>D-1024 | 2026-09-30 | No recap of the day on an evening return, and no Today you installed Docker in the goodnight. | The user knows what they did, and the desk brief cut a card of the day's receipts for the same reason. | Cut | [voice-brief: Cut, and why](design/voice-brief.md#cut-and-why) | n/a |
| <a id="d-1025"></a>D-1025 | 2026-09-30 | No voice on the working line, receipts, errors, limits, sign-out, offline, put-back lines or narrated step verbs. | They are facts and safety marks, and the first brief names chatty status lines as the failure mode. | Cut | [voice-brief: Cut, and why](design/voice-brief.md#cut-and-why) | n/a |
| <a id="d-1026"></a>D-1026 | 2026-09-30 | No greeting on every unlock, no lock-screen greeting and no goodbye on restart. | They are filler, or hidden behind the lock. | Cut | [voice-brief: Cut, and why](design/voice-brief.md#cut-and-why) | n/a |
| <a id="d-1027"></a>D-1027 | 2026-09-30 | Users do not write their own voices (talk like a pirate); a model-only flavour can come later. | The local lines could not follow, so the character would vanish at every boot. | Cut | [voice-brief: Cut, and why](design/voice-brief.md#cut-and-why) | n/a |
| <a id="d-1028"></a>D-1028 | 2026-09-30 | The first brief's welcome-back line keeps its decided words and gains a name and a voice; it is the return greeting, built locally with no model call, and fades after a touch instead of after a few seconds. |  | Default | [voice-brief: Extends or changes the earlier briefs](design/voice-brief.md#extends-or-changes-the-earlier-briefs) | [In progress](roadmap.md) · [voice.md](architecture/voice.md) |
| <a id="d-1029"></a>D-1029 | 2026-09-30 | The first brief's list of what skips the model gains one word, voice; the shutdown answer gains a goodbye clause, and restart is unchanged. |  | Default | [voice-brief: Extends or changes the earlier briefs](design/voice-brief.md#extends-or-changes-the-earlier-briefs) | [In progress](roadmap.md) · [voice.md](architecture/voice.md) |
| <a id="d-1030"></a>D-1030 | 2026-09-30 | Ask anything stays the resting placeholder in every voice, agent text stays one line while working and at most four after, and error wording is unchanged; the voice may not lengthen any of it. |  | Default | [voice-brief: Extends or changes the earlier briefs](design/voice-brief.md#extends-or-changes-the-earlier-briefs) | [Shipped](roadmap.md) · [shell.qml](../shell/shell.qml) |
| <a id="d-1031"></a>D-1031 | 2026-09-30 | Riding with Bombadil's counts for the line (updates with their security count, promises that came true) are fact clauses here, a page glyph can ride at the line's end, and its explain level stays separate from the voice. |  | Default | [voice-brief: Extends or changes the earlier briefs](design/voice-brief.md#extends-or-changes-the-earlier-briefs) | [In progress](roadmap.md) · [voice.md](architecture/voice.md) |

| <a id="d-1208"></a>D-1208 | 2026-09-30 | A person moves to a new laptop through the copy drive plus the recipe, not through a laptop-to-laptop network transfer. | The same path also covers a dead SSD or a stolen laptop. | Default | [installed-os-brief: Decisions I picked a default for](design/installed-os-brief.md#decisions-i-picked-a-default-for)<br>[installed-os-brief: Cut, and why](design/installed-os-brief.md#cut-and-why) | [Designed](roadmap.md) · [installed-os.md](architecture/installed-os.md) |
| <a id="d-1209"></a>D-1209 | 2026-09-30 | "Keep a copy on my USB drive" becomes an Autopilot routine: @home from a fresh snapshot, the recipe, the package lists and the sign-in, locked with the person's password (restic unverified). |  | Default | [installed-os-brief: After these, in order](design/installed-os-brief.md#after-these-in-order) | [Designed](roadmap.md) · [installed-os.md](architecture/installed-os.md) |
| <a id="d-1210"></a>D-1210 | 2026-09-30 | On a new laptop the stick finds the copy drive, installs stock Bombadil with this laptop's drivers, restores the home and replays the recipe as one turn, skipping what belonged to the old machine with a reason per line. |  | Default | [installed-os-brief: After these, in order](design/installed-os-brief.md#after-these-in-order) | [Designed](roadmap.md) · [installed-os.md](architecture/installed-os.md) |
| <a id="d-1211"></a>D-1211 | 2026-09-30 | The copy-and-restore path also adds encryption to an install made without it. | Encryption cannot be added under an existing btrfs without re-encrypting in place. | Default | [installed-os-brief: After these, in order](design/installed-os-brief.md#after-these-in-order) | [Designed](roadmap.md) · [installed-os.md](architecture/installed-os.md) |
| <a id="d-1212"></a>D-1212 | 2026-09-30 | "turn Secure Boot back on" is a red turn: sbctl with Microsoft's keys kept (-m), GRUB with --disable-shim-lock and SBAT, only after BitLocker is suspended. |  | Default | [installed-os-brief: After these, in order](design/installed-os-brief.md#after-these-in-order) | [Designed](roadmap.md) · [installed-os.md](architecture/installed-os.md) |
| <a id="d-1213"></a>D-1213 | 2026-09-30 | TPM unlock comes after Secure Boot, through GRUB's TPM2 key protector, with a GRUB console password; it is tested with swtpm first. | Otherwise an unlocked disk is one command away (inferred); some ThinkPads and ASUS TUF laptops can be bricked by replaced platform keys. | Default | [installed-os-brief: After these, in order](design/installed-os-brief.md#after-these-in-order) | [Designed](roadmap.md) · [installed-os.md](architecture/installed-os.md) |
| <a id="d-1214"></a>D-1214 | 2026-09-30 | A Bombadil you carry is an install onto a second USB drive from the same card: fallback path and initramfs, open GPU drivers only, hostname bombadil-stick, and a card note that a USB drive is slower and easier to lose. | A USB drive is slower and easier to lose, which is one more reason for encryption. | Default | [installed-os-brief: After these, in order](design/installed-os-brief.md#after-these-in-order) | [Designed](roadmap.md) · [installed-os.md](architecture/installed-os.md) |
| <a id="d-1215"></a>D-1215 | 2026-09-30 | An update is as the passenger brief designed, with Bombadil's own lines in the receipt; a new kernel ends with "Restart to finish" and undo takes it back too. |  | Default | [installed-os-brief: A life with an installed Bombadil](design/installed-os-brief.md#a-life-with-an-installed-bombadil) | [Designed](roadmap.md) · [installed-os.md](architecture/installed-os.md) |
| <a id="d-1216"></a>D-1216 | 2026-09-30 | New installs lock the disk with one password: the person types it when the computer starts, the same password unlocks the screen, and a recovery key is shown once. | The agent has sudo and accounts are signed in, so a lost laptop is worth more to a thief; Windows 11, macOS, Pop!_OS and Omarchy encrypt by default. | Confirmed | [installed-os-brief: Questions only you can answer](design/installed-os-brief.md#questions-only-you-can-answer) | [In progress](roadmap.md) · [installed-os.md](architecture/installed-os.md) |
| <a id="d-1217"></a>D-1217 | 2026-09-30 | A flash drive is the try, install and repair stick, plus a full Bombadil installed onto a USB drive the person carries between computers, with undo and updates like any install. | It comes almost free with piece 1 (the start file every PC finds, a start image for any hardware) and piece 3 (USB drives offered as targets). | Confirmed | [installed-os-brief: Questions only you can answer](design/installed-os-brief.md#questions-only-you-can-answer) | [Designed](roadmap.md) · [installed-os.md](architecture/installed-os.md) |
| <a id="d-1218"></a>D-1218 | 2026-09-30 | People installing on a whole disk or a spare laptop install first; installing next to Windows comes after. | It proves the disk layout and the update path before BitLocker, Fast Startup and Windows' small start partition enter the picture. | Confirmed | [installed-os-brief: Questions only you can answer](design/installed-os-brief.md#questions-only-you-can-answer) | [Designed](roadmap.md) · [installed-os.md](architecture/installed-os.md) |
| <a id="d-1219"></a>D-1219 | 2026-09-30 | CI signs every merge that passes the VM smoke test and publishes it to a testing channel, which the VM that mirrors a real install follows; the stable channel takes it after 3 days with no revert. | Nothing waits on a manual step, and daily use of the testing channel catches what the smoke test misses. | Default | [installed-os-brief: Decisions I picked a default for](design/installed-os-brief.md#decisions-i-picked-a-default-for) | [Designed](roadmap.md) · [installed-os.md](architecture/installed-os.md) |
| <a id="d-1220"></a>D-1220 | 2026-09-30 | Replaces [D-1268](#d-1268): The time zone is detected and never asked: on the stick, when the network first comes up, one HTTPS geo-IP request with no model sets it, and timesyncd sets the clock. | Even the stick's greeting can then say "Good morning". | Default | [installed-os-brief: Decisions I picked a default for](design/installed-os-brief.md#decisions-i-picked-a-default-for) | [Designed](roadmap.md) · [installed-os.md](architecture/installed-os.md) |
| <a id="d-1221"></a>D-1221 | 2026-09-30 | The card shows the zone as one line with "change", and the installer records timezone_source as detected, chosen or unset; an installed machine still unset does the lookup once at its first network. | Geo-IP guesses wrong in countries with several zones and behind VPNs. | Default | [installed-os-brief: Decisions I picked a default for](design/installed-os-brief.md#decisions-i-picked-a-default-for) | [Designed](roadmap.md) · [installed-os.md](architecture/installed-os.md) |
| <a id="d-1222"></a>D-1222 | 2026-09-30 | One password serves the disk, the account, the lock screen and the consoles, and is set even without encryption. | With passwordless sudo, an empty password on tty2 is root for anyone at the keyboard. | Default | [installed-os-brief: Decisions I picked a default for](design/installed-os-brief.md#decisions-i-picked-a-default-for) | [Designed](roadmap.md) · [installed-os.md](architecture/installed-os.md) |
| <a id="d-1223"></a>D-1223 | 2026-09-30 | The account stays user (UID 1000) and the agent's sudo stays passwordless; the person's name lives in persona.toml. | Every path, unit and test uses /home/user. | Default | [installed-os-brief: Decisions I picked a default for](design/installed-os-brief.md#decisions-i-picked-a-default-for) | [Designed](roadmap.md) · [installed-os.md](architecture/installed-os.md) |
| <a id="d-1224"></a>D-1224 | 2026-09-30 | Keyboard and language come from the stick's session; the computer name comes from the DMI model with the person's name in front when the voice card has one, else "bombadil-" and four characters of the machine id. | A fixed "bombadil" collides on a shared network. | Default | [installed-os-brief: Decisions I picked a default for](design/installed-os-brief.md#decisions-i-picked-a-default-for) | [Designed](roadmap.md) · [installed-os.md](architecture/installed-os.md) |
| <a id="d-1225"></a>D-1225 | 2026-09-30 | The hardware clock is UTC on a whole disk and local time next to Windows, said on the card; next to Windows this changes the passenger brief's UTC clause. | Writing RealTimeIsUniversal into Windows' SYSTEM hive from Linux risks the one file Windows needs, and BitLocker usually hides it. | Default | [installed-os-brief: Decisions I picked a default for](design/installed-os-brief.md#decisions-i-picked-a-default-for)<br>[installed-os-brief: Extends or changes the earlier briefs](design/installed-os-brief.md#extends-or-changes-the-earlier-briefs) | [Designed](roadmap.md) · [installed-os.md](architecture/installed-os.md) |
| <a id="d-1226"></a>D-1226 | 2026-09-30 | Installing before sign-in or offline is possible through the word "install"; the agent fills the card and the person presses the button. | A laptop whose Wi-Fi has no driver still needs a way in. | Default | [installed-os-brief: Decisions I picked a default for](design/installed-os-brief.md#decisions-i-picked-a-default-for) | [In progress](roadmap.md) · [installed-os.md](architecture/installed-os.md) |

| <a id="d-1419"></a>D-1419 | 2026-09-27 | Arch is paired with btrfs and snapper: a snapshot is taken before every agent turn that touches the system, as an undo button instead of a permission wall. | The no-guards goal needs an undo button instead of a permission wall. Changed in part by [D-1422](#d-1422). | Confirmed | [foundation-choices: 1. Base distro → Arch](design/foundation-choices.md#1-base-distro--arch) | [Shipped](roadmap.md) · [snapshots.py](../src/bombadil/snapshots.py) |
| <a id="d-1420"></a>D-1420 | 2026-09-27 | Any old snapshot can be booted from the bootloader. | It is the undo that replaces a permission wall. Narrowed by [D-1513](#d-1513). | Superseded by [D-1540](#d-1540) | [foundation-choices: 1. Base distro → Arch](design/foundation-choices.md#1-base-distro--arch) | [Designed](roadmap.md) · [bombadil-rollback](../iso/airootfs/usr/local/bin/bombadil-rollback) |
| <a id="d-1421"></a>D-1421 | 2026-09-27 | A visible activity log is part of the safety net, beside the btrfs snapshot per turn. | With no guards, the safety net is the snapshot per turn plus a visible record of what ran. | Confirmed | [foundation-choices: 4. How the agent runs as the OS → CLIs: official agent CLIs as the engine, our own daemon around them](design/foundation-choices.md#4-how-the-agent-runs-as-the-os--clis-official-agent-clis-as-the-engine-our-own-daemon-around-them) | [Shipped](roadmap.md) · [agentd.py](../src/bombadil/agentd.py) |
| <a id="d-1422"></a>D-1422 | 2026-09-27 | A restore point is taken before every turn, not only before turns that touch the system. | Most turns change nothing, and a full disk is a failure you would have to debug yourself. | Confirmed | [foundation-choices: AI Linux distro: foundation choices](design/foundation-choices.md#ai-linux-distro-foundation-choices)<br>[ux-brief: Decisions I picked a default for](design/ux-brief.md#decisions-i-picked-a-default-for) | [Shipped](roadmap.md) · [agentd.py](../src/bombadil/agentd.py) |
| <a id="d-1423"></a>D-1423 | 2026-09-27 | There are no permission prompts and nothing is hidden: safety comes from every change showing in plain words as it happens, and from undo saying exactly what it covered. | With no guard rails, safety comes from two things: every change is shown as it happens, and undo says what it covered. Narrowed by [D-1457](#d-1457). | Default | [ux-brief: The rules](design/ux-brief.md#the-rules) | [Partly shipped](roadmap.md) · [narrate.py](../src/bombadil/narrate.py) |
| <a id="d-1424"></a>D-1424 | 2026-09-27 | Recovery never goes through the part that broke: stop, undo and rescue must work without the model, the network or the GUI. | The agent can edit the shell, the compositor config and agentd itself. | Default | [ux-brief: The rules](design/ux-brief.md#the-rules) | [Partly shipped](roadmap.md) · [launcher.py](../src/bombadil/launcher.py) |
| <a id="d-1425"></a>D-1425 | 2026-09-27 | Esc in the pill, Super+Escape from anywhere or a click on the orange dot ends the turn and everything it started, sudo installs included, and the line says "Stopped while installing docker." | Each turn's CLI runs in its own systemd scope, so Stop can kill the whole group. Narrowed by [D-142](#d-142). | Default | [ux-brief: 2. The pill is the launcher, and Stop means stop](design/ux-brief.md#2-the-pill-is-the-launcher-and-stop-means-stop) | [Shipped](roadmap.md) · [procs.py](../src/bombadil/procs.py) |
| <a id="d-1426"></a>D-1426 | 2026-09-27 | Undo is handled locally by agentd, not through the agent; until live undo exists it says plainly that system files come back on the next restart. | Undo sent through the agent first took a snapshot of itself, then rolled back to that same snapshot, so it did nothing. | Default | [ux-brief: 2. The pill is the launcher, and Stop means stop](design/ux-brief.md#2-the-pill-is-the-launcher-and-stop-means-stop) | [Shipped](roadmap.md) · [launcher.py](../src/bombadil/launcher.py) |
| <a id="d-1427"></a>D-1427 | 2026-09-27 | Undo is live for apps (git), for home files (a /home snapper config, only files the turn changed) and for /etc-only changes; packages, /usr, /var/lib, kernel and /boot come back on restart. It never rolls back all of /home. | A short list of paths safe to revert live is safer than a list of exceptions, which would miss daemon state under /var/lib. Changed in part by [D-1157](#d-1157). | Confirmed | [ux-brief: Decisions I picked a default for](design/ux-brief.md#decisions-i-picked-a-default-for) | [Designed](roadmap.md) |
| <a id="d-1428"></a>D-1428 | 2026-09-27 | There is no pause before things no restore point can undo: the step turns red with its exact command, Esc stops it, and the closing line says "can't be undone". Extended by [D-1499](#d-1499), [D-538](#d-538). | Confirmed | [ux-brief: Decisions I picked a default for](design/ux-brief.md#decisions-i-picked-a-default-for) | [Partly shipped](roadmap.md) · [StatusLine.qml](../shell/StatusLine.qml) |
| <a id="d-1429"></a>D-1429 | 2026-09-27 | A change that breaks the display, the shell or the network reverts by itself, but only when a health check fails; agentd puts the changed files back and one line offers "Redo it". | A "does everything look right?" countdown after every restyle would interrupt the thing people most enjoy doing. | Confirmed | [ux-brief: Decisions I picked a default for](design/ux-brief.md#decisions-i-picked-a-default-for) | [Designed](roadmap.md) |
| <a id="d-1430"></a>D-1430 | 2026-09-27 | The rescue screen is a text page in large type on tty2 with four choices (undo the last change, restart the desktop, talk to Claude here, open a shell), plus a panic key while the compositor still runs. | A text console depends on the fewest things that can break, and Ctrl+Alt+F2 still works when hyprland.lua is broken. Extended by [D-1262](#d-1262), [D-1496](#d-1496). Extended by [D-1451](#d-1451), [D-1489](#d-1489), [D-370](#d-370), [D-953](#d-953). | Default | [ux-brief: When it goes wrong](design/ux-brief.md#when-it-goes-wrong) | [Partly shipped](roadmap.md) · [providers.py](../src/bombadil/providers.py) |
